Your chat line is now on.
This hall is where new adventurers seeking fame and fortune in the world of
Ancient Anguish learn the absolute basics. It is within a house in the
residential section of Tantallon, the major city of the continent of Anguish.
You may go east to another similar looking room or type 'leave' to finish
these lessons. 
 The only obvious exit is east.
Leila, an elven woman.
> Leila says to you: You can now go east to get to your next lesson. Or you
                   could just 'leave' the tutorial.
leave

Type 'leave' again if you really want to leave.
> leave\

> leave
Confident in your abilities, you end your lessons and leave the house.
You stand on a small residential street of Tantallon. Large houses rise high
over the street to the north and south all along this street running west
into the dense residential area of the village. To the east you can see the
shoreline road. There is a closed oak door leading north and a closed spruce
door leading south.
 Obvious directions are:
  east, west.

You are within a public house favoured by the serpentari lower classes. The
adobe walls are adorned with primitive-looking paintings, and the windows are
shrouded by ragged curtains. Low, round tables are placed about the pub for
use by its patrons; one table, in a back corner, has an oblong shape atop it.
A menu is posted on one wall; the cuisine here seems prepared specifically to
delight the serpentari palate. The air is hazy with tobacco smoke. 
 The only obvious exit is southwest.

You are within a public house favoured by the serpentari lower classes. The
adobe walls are adorned with primitive-looking paintings, and the windows are
shrouded by ragged curtains. Low, round tables are placed about the pub for
use by its patrons; one table, in a back corner, has an oblong shape atop it.
A menu is posted on one wall; the cuisine here seems prepared specifically to
delight the serpentari palate. The air is hazy with tobacco smoke. 
 The only obvious exit is southwest.

stand just west of you.

decorated rug, upon which are strewn huge stuffed cushions. A matched pair of

A tiny clearing (s)
1,235
390/390:201>
X:[-1]Y:[16]
l
You come upon a small clearing in the thicket. The floor of the clearing is
covered with tiny flowers of every colour. In the centre is a fairy ring - a
circle formed of toadstools. A tiny path leads up to the ring and seems to
'enter' it. A glistening dew covers everything, and it is strangely silent
here.  A narrowing passage leads south through the thicket back to the
forest. 
 The only obvious exit is south.
